import { Routes, Route } from 'react-router-dom';
import Landing from './pages/Landing';
import Dashboard from './pages/Dashboard';
import Studio from './pages/Studio';

function App() {
  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route path="/" element={<Landing />} />
      <Route path="/dashboard" element={<Dashboard />} />
      <Route path="/notebook/:id" element={<Studio />} />
    </Routes>
  );
}

export default App;

# FILE: frontend/src/components/DocCompareModal.jsx
# ==============================================================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { X, Scale, FileText, Check, Copy, Download, Loader, Sparkles, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import MarkdownRenderer from './MarkdownRenderer';

export default function DocCompareModal({ 
  isOpen, 
  onClose, 
  documents = [], 
  onCompare, 
  comparisonResult, 
  comparing, 
  isDark = true 
}) {
  const [selectedDocs, setSelectedDocs] = useState([]);
  const [focusTopic, setFocusTopic] = useState('');
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const toggleDoc = (filename) => {
    setSelectedDocs(prev => 
      prev.includes(filename) ? prev.filter(f => f !== filename) : [...prev, filename]
    );
  };

  const selectAll = () => {
    setSelectedDocs(documents.map(d => d.filename));
  };

  const handleStartCompare = () => {
    if (selectedDocs.length < 2) return;
    onCompare(selectedDocs, focusTopic);
  };

  const handleCopy = () => {
    if (!comparisonResult) return;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(comparisonResult);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
  };

  const handleDownload = () => {
    if (!comparisonResult) return;
    const blob = new Blob([comparisonResult], { type: 'text/markdown;charset=utf-8' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `so-sanh-tai-lieu-${Date.now()}.md`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-md p-4 animate-fade-in font-sans">
      <div className={`w-full max-w-4xl h-[88vh] flex flex-col rounded-3xl shadow-2xl border overflow-hidden transition-colors ${
        isDark ? 'bg-[#14151a] border-white/15 text-white' : 'bg-white border-gray-200 text-gray-900'
      }`}>
        {/* Header */}
        <div className={`p-4 border-b flex items-center justify-between ${
          isDark ? 'bg-white/[0.02] border-white/10' : 'bg-gray-50 border-gray-200'
        }`}>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="p-2 bg-gradient-to-tr from-amber-500 to-orange-600 rounded-xl text-white shadow-md">
              <Scale size={20} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-base font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
                So sánh & Đối chiếu Tài liệu
                <span className="text-[11px] font-normal px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/30">
                  Multi-Doc Analysis
                </span>
              </h2>
              <p className={`text-xs ${isDark ? 'text-gray-400' : 'text-gray-500'}`}>
                Đối chiếu tương đồng, dị biệt và tổng hợp luận điểm giữa nhiều nguồn tài liệu
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className={`p-2 rounded-xl transition ${isDark ? 'hover:bg-white/10 text-gray-400 hover:text-white' : 'hover:bg-gray-100 text-gray-500 hover:text-black'}`}
          >
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Document Selection Strip */}
        <div className={`p-4 border-b space-y-3 ${
          isDark ? 'bg-black/20 border-white/10' : 'bg-gray-50/70 border-gray-200'
        }`}>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-300 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span>Chọn tài liệu đối chiếu (Tối thiểu 2):</span>
              <span className="text-amber-400 font-mono">({selectedDocs.length}/{documents.length})</span>
            </span>
            <button
              onClick={selectAll}
              className="text-[11px] text-blue-400 hover:underline font-medium"
            >
              Chọn tất cả
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Doc Pills */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 max-h-24 overflow-y-auto scrollbar-hide">
            {documents.length === 0 ? (
              <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 italic">Chưa có tài liệu nào trong sổ tay để so sánh.</span>
            ) : (
              documents.map(doc => {
                const isSelected = selectedDocs.includes(doc.filename);
                return (
                  <button
                    key={doc.filename}
                    onClick={() => toggleDoc(doc.filename)}
                    type="button"
                    className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium border flex items-center gap-2 transition ${
                      isSelected 
                        ? 'bg-amber-500/20 border-amber-500/50 text-amber-200 shadow-sm'
                        : isDark ? 'bg-white/5 border-white/10 text-gray-400 hover:text-white' : 'bg-white border-gray-200 text-gray-600 hover:text-black'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded flex items-center justify-center border text-[9px] ${
                      isSelected ? 'bg-amber-500 border-amber-500 text-black font-bold' : 'border-gray-500'
                    }`}>
                      {isSelected && <Check size={10} />}
                    </div>
                    <FileText size={13} className="shrink-0" />
                    <span className="truncate max-w-[200px]">{doc.filename}</span>
                  </button>
                );
              })
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Topic & Compare Button */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-1">
            <div className="flex-1 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Sparkles size={14} className="text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
              <input
                type="text"
                value={focusTopic}
                onChange={e => setFocusTopic(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Tiêu chí hoặc câu hỏi trọng tâm muốn đối chiếu (tùy chọn)..."
                className={`w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs border outline-none transition ${
                  isDark ? 'bg-white/5 border-white/10 text-white focus:border-amber-500' : 'bg-white border-gray-200 text-black focus:border-amber-600'
                }`}
              />
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={handleStartCompare}
              disabled={selectedDocs.length < 2 || comparing}
              className="bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-orange-600 hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-40 text-black font-bold px-5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs shadow-lg transition flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0"
            >
              {comparing ? (
                <>
                  <Loader size={14} className="animate-spin text-black" />
                  <span>Đang phân tích đối chiếu...</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Scale size={14} />
                  <span>So sánh {selectedDocs.length} tài liệu</span>
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Content Body */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 scrollbar-hide">
          {comparing ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full gap-3 text-gray-400">
              <Loader size={30} className="animate-spin text-amber-400" />
              <p className="text-sm font-medium">AI đang trích xuất dữ liệu và xây dựng bảng đối chiếu chuyên sâu...</p>
            </div>
          ) : !comparisonResult ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full gap-3 text-gray-400 max-w-md mx-auto text-center">
              <Scale size={42} className="text-amber-500/60" />
              <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">Chưa có kết quả đối chiếu</h4>
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 leading-relaxed">
                Hãy tích chọn ít nhất 2 tài liệu ở trên và nhấn nút <span className="text-amber-400 font-semibold">"So sánh tài liệu"</span> để nhận phân tích chi tiết từ AI.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-4 max-w-3xl mx-auto">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-white/10">
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-amber-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
                  Kết quả Phân tích Đối chiếu
                </span>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <button
                    onClick={handleCopy}
                    className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-gray-300 transition"
                  >
                    {copied ? <Check size={13} className="text-green-400" /> : <Copy size={13} />}
                    <span>{copied ? "Đã chép" : "Sao chép"}</span>
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={handleDownload}
                    className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-gray-300 transition"
                  >
                    <Download size={13} />
                    <span>Tải về .md</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="p-2">
                <MarkdownRenderer content={comparisonResult} isDark={isDark} />
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


# ==============================================================================
# FILE: frontend/src/components/MarkdownRenderer.jsx
# ==============================================================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
import remarkGfm from 'remark-gfm';
import { Copy, Check } from 'lucide-react';

function CodeBlock({ children, className, ...props }) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const match = /language-(\w+)/.exec(className || '');
  const language = match ? match[1] : '';
  const codeText = String(children).replace(/\n$/, '');

  const handleCopy = () => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(codeText);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
  };

  return (
    <div className="relative my-3 rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-white/10 bg-[#0d0e12] font-mono text-xs">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-white/[0.04] border-b border-white/10 text-gray-400 text-[11px]">
        <span className="font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">{language || 'code'}</span>
        <button
          onClick={handleCopy}
          type="button"
          className="flex items-center gap-1 hover:text-white transition px-2 py-0.5 rounded hover:bg-white/10"
          title="Sao chép mã"
        >
          {copied ? (
            <>
              <Check size={12} className="text-green-400" />
              <span className="text-green-400">Đã chép</span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <Copy size={12} />
              <span>Sao chép</span>
            </>
          )}
        </button>
      </div>
      <div className="p-3.5 overflow-x-auto text-gray-200 leading-relaxed scrollbar-hide">
        <code>{codeText}</code>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function MarkdownRenderer({ content, isDark = true }) {
  if (!content) return null;

  return (
    <div className={`markdown-content text-sm leading-relaxed ${isDark ? 'text-gray-200' : 'text-gray-800'}`}>
      <ReactMarkdown
        remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}
        components={{
          code({ node, inline, className, children, ...props }) {
            const hasLang = /language-(\w+)/.test(className || '');
            const isMultiline = String(children).includes('\n');
            if (!inline && (hasLang || isMultiline)) {
              return <CodeBlock className={className} {...props}>{children}</CodeBlock>;
            }
            return (
              <code 
                className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[12px] font-mono font-medium ${
                  isDark ? 'bg-white/10 text-pink-300' : 'bg-gray-200 text-purple-700'
                }`} 
                {...props}
              >
                {children}
              </code>
            );
          },
          h1: ({ children }) => (
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold mt-4 mb-2 pb-1 border-b border-white/10">{children}</h1>
          ),
          h2: ({ children }) => (
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mt-3 mb-1.5 text-blue-400">{children}</h2>
          ),
          h3: ({ children }) => (
            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold mt-2.5 mb-1 text-purple-400">{children}</h3>
          ),
          p: ({ children }) => (
            <p className="my-1.5 leading-relaxed">{children}</p>
          ),
          ul: ({ children }) => (
            <ul className="list-disc list-inside my-2 space-y-1 pl-2">{children}</ul>
          ),
          ol: ({ children }) => (
            <ol className="list-decimal list-inside my-2 space-y-1 pl-2">{children}</ol>
          ),
          li: ({ children }) => (
            <li className="leading-relaxed">{children}</li>
          ),
          blockquote: ({ children }) => (
            <blockquote className="border-l-4 border-purple-500/80 pl-3 my-2 italic text-gray-400 bg-white/[0.02] py-1 rounded-r">
              {children}
            </blockquote>
          ),
          table: ({ children }) => (
            <div className="my-3 overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border border-white/15 scrollbar-hide">
              <table className="w-full text-xs text-left border-collapse">{children}</table>
            </div>
          ),
          thead: ({ children }) => (
            <thead className="bg-white/10 text-white font-semibold border-b border-white/15">{children}</thead>
          ),
          th: ({ children }) => (
            <th className="px-3 py-2 border-r border-white/10 last:border-r-0">{children}</th>
          ),
          td: ({ children }) => (
            <td className="px-3 py-2 border-t border-white/10 border-r last:border-r-0">{children}</td>
          ),
          a: ({ href, children }) => (
            <a 
              href={href} 
              target="_blank" 
              rel="noopener noreferrer" 
              className="text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300 underline underline-offset-2 transition"
            >
              {children}
            </a>
          )
        }}
      >
        {content}
      </ReactMarkdown>
    </div>
  );
}


# ==============================================================================
# FILE: frontend/src/components/MindmapModal.jsx
# ==============================================================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { X, ZoomIn, ZoomOut, RotateCcw, Copy, Check, Sparkles, Network, RefreshCw, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';

export default function MindmapModal({ isOpen, onClose, mindmapData, onRegenerate, loading, isDark = true }) {
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
  const [selectedNode, setSelectedNode] = useState(null);
  const [copiedMermaid, setCopiedMermaid] = useState(false);
  const [customTopic, setCustomTopic] = useState('');

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleZoomIn = () => setZoom(prev => Math.min(prev + 0.15, 2));
  const handleZoomOut = () => setZoom(prev => Math.max(prev - 0.15, 0.5));
  const handleResetZoom = () => setZoom(1);

  // Generate mermaid representation
  const generateMermaid = (node) => {
    if (!node) return '';
    let res = 'graph TD\n';
    const sanitize = (str) => (str || '').replace(/["()[\]{}]/g, '');
    const traverse = (parent) => {
      if (!parent.children) return;
      parent.children.forEach(child => {
        res += `  ${sanitize(parent.id)}["${sanitize(parent.name)}"] --> ${sanitize(child.id)}["${sanitize(child.name)}"]\n`;
        traverse(child);
      });
    };
    traverse(node);
    return res;
  };

  const handleCopyMermaid = () => {
    if (!mindmapData) return;
    const mermaid = generateMermaid(mindmapData);
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(mermaid);
    setCopiedMermaid(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopiedMermaid(false), 2000);
  };

  const branches = mindmapData?.children || [];

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-md p-4 animate-fade-in font-sans">
      <div className={`w-full max-w-5xl h-[88vh] flex flex-col rounded-3xl shadow-2xl border overflow-hidden transition-colors ${
        isDark ? 'bg-[#121316] border-white/15 text-white' : 'bg-white border-gray-200 text-gray-900'
      }`}>
        {/* Header */}
        <div className={`p-4 border-b flex items-center justify-between ${
          isDark ? 'bg-white/[0.02] border-white/10' : 'bg-gray-50 border-gray-200'
        }`}>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="p-2 bg-gradient-to-tr from-purple-600 to-blue-600 rounded-xl text-white shadow-md">
              <Network size={20} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-base font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
                Sơ đồ tư duy & Bản đồ tri thức (Mind Map)
                <span className="text-[11px] font-normal px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-400 border border-purple-500/30">
                  AI Generated
                </span>
              </h2>
              <p className={`text-xs ${isDark ? 'text-gray-400' : 'text-gray-500'}`}>
                Trực quan hóa các mối quan hệ khái niệm cốt lõi trong tài liệu của bạn
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {/* Zoom controls */}
            <div className={`flex items-center gap-1 border rounded-xl p-1 ${
              isDark ? 'border-white/10 bg-black/30' : 'border-gray-200 bg-gray-100'
            }`}>
              <button 
                onClick={handleZoomOut} 
                className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg transition ${isDark ? 'hover:bg-white/10 text-gray-300' : 'hover:bg-gray-200 text-gray-700'}`}
                title="Thu nhỏ"
              >
                <ZoomOut size={15} />
              </button>
              <span className="text-[11px] font-mono px-1 min-w-[40px] text-center font-semibold">
                {Math.round(zoom * 100)}%
              </span>
              <button 
                onClick={handleZoomIn} 
                className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg transition ${isDark ? 'hover:bg-white/10 text-gray-300' : 'hover:bg-gray-200 text-gray-700'}`}
                title="Phóng to"
              >
                <ZoomIn size={15} />
              </button>
              <button 
                onClick={handleResetZoom} 
                className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg transition ${isDark ? 'hover:bg-white/10 text-gray-300' : 'hover:bg-gray-200 text-gray-700'}`}
                title="Đặt lại zoom"
              >
                <RotateCcw size={15} />
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Copy Mermaid */}
            <button
              onClick={handleCopyMermaid}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition ${
                isDark ? 'border-white/15 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-gray-200' : 'border-gray-200 bg-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-200 text-gray-700'
              }`}
              title="Sao chép sơ đồ dưới dạng mã Mermaid"
            >
              {copiedMermaid ? <Check size={14} className="text-green-400" /> : <Copy size={14} />}
              <span>{copiedMermaid ? "Đã chép Mermaid" : "Xuất Mermaid"}</span>
            </button>

            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className={`p-2 rounded-xl transition ${isDark ? 'hover:bg-white/10 text-gray-400 hover:text-white' : 'hover:bg-gray-100 text-gray-500 hover:text-black'}`}
            >
              <X size={20} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Action Bar for Regenerating */}
        <div className={`px-5 py-2.5 border-b flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs ${
          isDark ? 'bg-black/30 border-white/10' : 'bg-gray-50 border-gray-200'
        }`}>
          <div className="flex-1 flex items-center gap-2 max-w-lg">
            <Sparkles size={14} className="text-purple-400 shrink-0" />
            <input
              type="text"
              value={customTopic}
              onChange={e => setCustomTopic(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Chủ đề trọng tâm cần tập trung tạo sơ đồ (tùy chọn)..."
              className={`w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs border outline-none transition ${
                isDark ? 'bg-white/5 border-white/10 text-white focus:border-purple-500' : 'bg-white border-gray-200 text-black focus:border-purple-600'
              }`}
            />
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => onRegenerate(customTopic)}
            disabled={loading}
            className="bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600 to-blue-600 hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-50 text-white px-4 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold shadow transition flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0"
          >
            <RefreshCw size={13} className={loading ? "animate-spin" : ""} />
            <span>{loading ? "Đang tạo sơ đồ..." : "Tái tạo Sơ đồ"}</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Content Canvas */}
        <div className="flex-1 relative overflow-auto p-8 select-none flex items-start justify-center">
          {loading ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full gap-3 text-gray-400">
              <RefreshCw size={28} className="animate-spin text-purple-400" />
              <p className="text-sm font-medium">AI đang phân tích tài liệu và dựng cây sơ đồ tri thức...</p>
            </div>
          ) : !mindmapData ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full gap-3 text-gray-400">
              <Network size={40} className="text-gray-500" />
              <p className="text-sm">Chưa có dữ liệu sơ đồ tư duy cho sổ tay này.</p>
              <button
                onClick={() => onRegenerate()}
                className="bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-700 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold"
              >
                Tạo sơ đồ tư duy ngay
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div 
              style={{ transform: `scale(${zoom})`, transformOrigin: 'top center', transition: 'transform 0.2s ease-out' }}
              className="w-full max-w-4xl flex flex-col items-center gap-8 py-6"
            >
              {/* Root Node */}
              <div 
                onClick={() => setSelectedNode(mindmapData)}
                className="cursor-pointer group relative p-5 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600 via-indigo-600 to-blue-600 text-white shadow-xl shadow-purple-500/20 max-w-md text-center hover:scale-105 transition transform border border-white/20"
              >
                <div className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-purple-200 font-bold mb-1">Chủ đề Trung tâm</div>
                <h3 className="text-lg font-extrabold">{mindmapData.name || "Chủ đề chính"}</h3>
                {mindmapData.desc && (
                  <p className="text-xs text-purple-100/90 mt-1 line-clamp-2">{mindmapData.desc}</p>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Connecting curve indicator */}
              <div className="w-0.5 h-8 bg-gradient-to-b from-indigo-500 to-transparent"></div>

              {/* Level 1: Branches Grid */}
              <div className="w-full grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                {branches.map((branch, idx) => {
                  const colors = [
                    'from-blue-500/20 to-cyan-500/10 border-blue-500/30 text-blue-400',
                    'from-purple-500/20 to-pink-500/10 border-purple-500/30 text-purple-400',
                    'from-emerald-500/20 to-teal-500/10 border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-400',
                    'from-amber-500/20 to-orange-500/10 border-amber-500/30 text-amber-400',
                    'from-rose-500/20 to-red-500/10 border-rose-500/30 text-rose-400',
                  ];
                  const colorScheme = colors[idx % colors.length];

                  return (
                    <div 
                      key={branch.id || idx}
                      className={`rounded-2xl border p-4.5 bg-gradient-to-b ${colorScheme} backdrop-blur-sm shadow-lg flex flex-col justify-between`}
                    >
                      {/* Branch Header */}
                      <div>
                        <div 
                          onClick={() => setSelectedNode(branch)}
                          className="cursor-pointer group flex items-start justify-between gap-2 mb-2 pb-2 border-b border-white/10"
                        >
                          <div>
                            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider opacity-80">
                              Nhánh {idx + 1}
                            </span>
                            <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-white group-hover:text-blue-300 transition">
                              {branch.name}
                            </h4>
                          </div>
                          <ChevronRight size={16} className="text-gray-400 group-hover:translate-x-1 transition shrink-0 mt-1" />
                        </div>

                        {branch.desc && (
                          <p className="text-xs text-gray-300 mb-3 leading-relaxed">
                            {branch.desc}
                          </p>
                        )}
                      </div>

                      {/* Sub-branches */}
                      {branch.children && branch.children.length > 0 && (
                        <div className="space-y-2 mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-white/10">
                          <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold text-gray-400 tracking-wider">
                            Khái niệm chi tiết ({branch.children.length}):
                          </span>
                          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                            {branch.children.map((sub, sIdx) => (
                              <button
                                key={sub.id || sIdx}
                                onClick={() => setSelectedNode(sub)}
                                className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium bg-black/40 hover:bg-black/60 border border-white/10 text-gray-200 transition text-left flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm"
                              >
                                <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-blue-400"></span>
                                <span className="truncate max-w-[160px]">{sub.name}</span>
                              </button>
                            ))}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Node Detail Popup Drawer */}
          {selectedNode && (
            <div className={`absolute bottom-6 right-6 max-w-sm w-full p-4.5 rounded-2xl border shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-md animate-fade-in ${
              isDark ? 'bg-[#181a20]/95 border-purple-500/40 text-white' : 'bg-white/95 border-purple-400 text-gray-900'
            }`}>
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2 mb-2">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-purple-400">Chi tiết Khái niệm</span>
                  <h4 className="text-sm font-bold mt-0.5">{selectedNode.name}</h4>
                </div>
                <button 
                  onClick={() => setSelectedNode(null)}
                  className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white p-1 rounded-lg"
                >
                  <X size={15} />
                </button>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-300 leading-relaxed">
                {selectedNode.desc || "Không có mô tả chi tiết thêm cho nút này."}
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


# ==============================================================================
# FILE: frontend/src/components/ModelManagerModal.jsx
# ==============================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
  X, Cpu, Cloud, Download, Check, RefreshCw, Plus, Trash2, 
  Sparkles, AlertCircle, ArrowRight, HardDrive, Zap, Info, Loader
} from 'lucide-react';

export default function ModelManagerModal({ 
  isOpen, 
  onClose, 
  activeNotebook, 
  onSelectModel,
  initialTab = 'recommend'
}) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState(initialTab);
  const [recommendations, setRecommendations] = useState([]);
  const [localModels, setLocalModels] = useState([]);
  const [ollamaAvailable, setOllamaAvailable] = useState(false);
  const [loadingScan, setLoadingScan] = useState(false);
  
  // Trạng thái tải model qua Ollama
  const [pullingModel, setPullingModel] = useState(null);
  const [pullProgress, setPullProgress] = useState({ percent: 0, statusText: '', detail: '' });

  // Custom model form
  const [customProvider, setCustomProvider] = useState('ollama');
  const [customModelName, setCustomModelName] = useState('');
  const [customBaseUrl, setCustomBaseUrl] = useState('');
  const [customApiKey, setCustomApiKey] = useState('');
  const [savedCustomModels, setSavedCustomModels] = useState([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      setActiveTab(initialTab);
      loadAllData();
      loadCustomModels();
    }
  }, [isOpen, initialTab]);

  const loadCustomModels = () => {
    try {
      const stored = localStorage.getItem('custom_ai_models');
      if (stored) {
        setSavedCustomModels(JSON.parse(stored));
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
    }
  };

  const saveCustomModelsToStorage = (newList) => {
    setSavedCustomModels(newList);
    localStorage.setItem('custom_ai_models', JSON.stringify(newList));
  };

  const loadAllData = async () => {
    setLoadingScan(true);
    try {
      // 1. Quét recommendations
      const recRes = await fetch('/api/llm/recommendations');
      if (recRes.ok) {
        const recData = await recRes.json();
        setRecommendations(recData.recommendations || []);
        setOllamaAvailable(recData.ollama_available || false);
      }

      // 2. Quét local models
      const localRes = await fetch('/api/llm/local-models');
      if (localRes.ok) {
        const localData = await localRes.json();
        const oModels = localData?.ollama?.models || [];
        setLocalModels(oModels);
        if (localData?.ollama?.available) {
          setOllamaAvailable(true);
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Lỗi quét models:", e);
    } finally {
      setLoadingScan(false);
    }
  };

  // Bắt đầu kéo/tải model Ollama qua SSE
  const handleStartPull = async (modelName) => {
    if (pullingModel) {
      alert("Đang có tiến trình tải model khác, vui lòng đợi...");
      return;
    }

    setPullingModel(modelName);
    setPullProgress({ percent: 0, statusText: 'Đang kết nối tới Ollama...', detail: '' });

    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/llm/ollama/pull', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ name: modelName })
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`);
      }

      const reader = response.body.getReader();
      const decoder = new TextDecoder('utf-8');
      let buffer = '';

      while (true) {
        const { value, done } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;

        buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
        const events = buffer.split('\n\n');
        buffer = events.pop() || '';

        for (const ev of events) {
          if (ev.startsWith('data: ')) {
            try {
              const data = JSON.parse(ev.substring(6));
              
              if (data.status === 'success') {
                setPullProgress({ percent: 100, statusText: 'Hoàn tất tải về!', detail: 'Đã sẵn sàng sử dụng.' });
                setTimeout(() => {
                  setPullingModel(null);
                  loadAllData();
                  // Tự động kích hoạt dùng model vừa tải
                  onSelectModel('ollama', modelName);
                }, 1200);
                return;
              }

              if (data.status === 'error') {
                alert("Lỗi tải model: " + (data.message || 'Không rõ nguyên nhân'));
                setPullingModel(null);
                return;
              }

              let pct = 0;
              let detailStr = data.status || '';
              if (data.total && data.completed) {
                pct = Math.round((data.completed / data.total) * 100);
                const mbCompleted = (data.completed / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1);
                const mbTotal = (data.total / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1);
                detailStr = `${mbCompleted} MB / ${mbTotal} MB (${pct}%)`;
              }

              setPullProgress({
                percent: pct,
                statusText: data.status || 'Đang tải...',
                detail: detailStr
              });
            } catch (err) {
              console.debug(err);
            }
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      alert("Lỗi kết nối khi tải model: " + e.message);
      setPullingModel(null);
    }
  };

  // Thêm custom model
  const handleAddCustomModel = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const trimmed = customModelName.trim();
    if (!trimmed) {
      alert("Vui lòng nhập tên model!");
      return;
    }

    const newModel = {
      id: Date.now().toString(),
      name: trimmed,
      provider: customProvider,
      model: trimmed,
      model_name: trimmed,
      base_url: customBaseUrl.trim() || undefined,
      api_key: customApiKey.trim() || undefined,
      created_at: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    const updated = [newModel, ...savedCustomModels.filter(m => m.model_name !== trimmed || m.provider !== customProvider)];
    saveCustomModelsToStorage(updated);
    
    // Áp dụng ngay
    onSelectModel(customProvider, trimmed, newModel.base_url, newModel.api_key);
    setCustomModelName('');
    setCustomBaseUrl('');
    setCustomApiKey('');
    onClose();
  };

  const handleDeleteCustomModel = (id, e) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    const updated = savedCustomModels.filter(m => m.id !== id);
    saveCustomModelsToStorage(updated);
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const currentProvider = (activeNotebook?.provider || (activeNotebook?.is_private ? 'ollama' : 'gemini')).toLowerCase();
  const currentModel = activeNotebook?.model_name || '';

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm animate-fade-in">
      <div className="bg-[#18191e] border border-white/10 rounded-2xl w-full max-w-2xl max-h-[85vh] flex flex-col shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">
        
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-white/10 flex items-center justify-between bg-white/[0.02]">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-green-500/20 to-blue-500/20 border border-green-500/30 flex items-center justify-center text-green-400">
              <Cpu size={20} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
                Quản lý & Tải Mô hình AI
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">
                Tự động quét model trên máy, gợi ý tải 1-click & thêm model tùy chỉnh
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              onClick={loadAllData}
              disabled={loadingScan}
              className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-gray-300 hover:text-white transition flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium"
              title="Quét lại các model trên máy tính"
            >
              <RefreshCw size={13} className={loadingScan ? "animate-spin text-blue-400" : ""} />
              <span className="hidden sm:inline">Quét lại</span>
            </button>
            <button 
              onClick={onClose} 
              className="p-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-white/10 text-gray-400 hover:text-white transition"
            >
              <X size={18} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Ollama Status Alert Banner */}
        <div className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-black/40 border-b border-white/5 flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${ollamaAvailable ? 'bg-green-400 animate-pulse' : 'bg-red-400'}`}></span>
            {ollamaAvailable ? (
              <span className="text-green-300 font-medium">
                Ollama đang chạy • Đã tìm thấy <strong>{localModels.length}</strong> mô hình trên máy bạn
              </span>
            ) : (
              <span className="text-amber-300 font-medium">
                Ollama chưa bật hoặc chưa cài đặt • Bạn có thể mở ứng dụng Ollama hoặc dùng 9Router Gateway
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
          <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 font-mono">
            {activeNotebook?.title ? `Sổ tay: "${activeNotebook.title}"` : ''}
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Tabs Bar */}
        <div className="flex border-b border-white/10 px-5 bg-white/[0.01]">
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('recommend')}
            className={`py-3 px-3 text-xs font-medium border-b-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition ${
              activeTab === 'recommend'
                ? 'border-green-500 text-green-400 font-semibold'
                : 'border-transparent text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-200'
            }`}
          >
            <Sparkles size={14} /> Gợi ý tải cho máy
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('installed')}
            className={`py-3 px-3 text-xs font-medium border-b-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition ${
              activeTab === 'installed'
                ? 'border-blue-500 text-blue-400 font-semibold'
                : 'border-transparent text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-200'
            }`}
          >
            <HardDrive size={14} /> Model trên máy ({localModels.length})
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('add_custom')}
            className={`py-3 px-3 text-xs font-medium border-b-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition ${
              activeTab === 'add_custom'
                ? 'border-purple-500 text-purple-400 font-semibold'
                : 'border-transparent text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-200'
            }`}
          >
            <Plus size={14} /> Thêm model tùy chỉnh
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Modal Body */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-5 space-y-4">
          
          {/* TAB 1: GỢI Ý TẢI MODEL CHO MÁY */}
          {activeTab === 'recommend' && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-blue-500/10 border border-blue-500/20 text-xs text-blue-300 flex items-start gap-2.5">
                <Info size={16} className="shrink-0 mt-0.5 text-blue-400" />
                <div className="leading-relaxed">
                  Các mô hình dưới đây được tối ưu riêng cho <strong>macOS Apple Silicon (M1/M2/M3/M4)</strong> với RAM từ 8GB đến 16GB.
                  Bấm <strong>Tải về máy</strong> để tải tự động qua Ollama và sử dụng hoàn toàn Offline bảo mật!
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Progress bar khi đang tải */}
              {pullingModel && (
                <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-green-500/10 to-blue-500/10 border border-green-500/30 space-y-2.5 animate-pulse">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-semibold">
                    <span className="text-green-300 flex items-center gap-2">
                      <Loader size={14} className="animate-spin text-green-400" />
                      Đang tải mô hình: <code className="text-white bg-black/40 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">{pullingModel}</code>
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-white font-mono">{pullProgress.percent}%</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="w-full bg-white/10 rounded-full h-2.5 overflow-hidden">
                    <div 
                      className="bg-gradient-to-r from-green-500 to-blue-500 h-2.5 rounded-full transition-all duration-300" 
                      style={{ width: `${pullProgress.percent}%` }}
                    ></div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-gray-400">
                    <span>{pullProgress.statusText}</span>
                    <span>{pullProgress.detail}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Danh sách model gợi ý */}
              <div className="space-y-3">
                {recommendations.map((rec) => {
                  const isCurrent = currentProvider === 'ollama' && (currentModel === rec.name || currentModel.startsWith(rec.name.split(':')[0]));
                  const isPullingThis = pullingModel === rec.name;

                  return (
                    <div 
                      key={rec.name}
                      className={`p-3.5 rounded-xl border transition-all ${
                        isCurrent 
                          ? 'bg-green-500/10 border-green-500/40 shadow-lg' 
                          : 'bg-white/[0.03] border-white/10 hover:border-white/20'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                        <div className="space-y-1 flex-1">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                            <h3 className="font-semibold text-sm text-white">{rec.displayName}</h3>
                            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono bg-white/10 text-gray-300 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">
                              {rec.name}
                            </span>
                            <span className="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 font-semibold border border-emerald-500/30">
                              {rec.badge}
                            </span>
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-amber-400">{rec.rating}</span>
                          </div>
                          
                          <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 leading-relaxed pt-1">
                            {rec.description}
                          </p>

                          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 pt-2 text-[11px] text-gray-400">
                            <span>Dung lượng: <strong className="text-gray-200">{rec.size}</strong></span>
                            <span>•</span>
                            <span>Yêu cầu RAM: <strong className="text-gray-200">{rec.min_ram}</strong></span>
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="shrink-0 flex flex-col items-end gap-2">
                          {rec.installed ? (
                            <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1.5">
                              <span className="text-[11px] text-green-400 font-medium flex items-center gap-1">
                                <Check size={12} strokeWidth={3} /> Đã có trên máy
                              </span>
                              <button
                                onClick={() => {
                                  onSelectModel('ollama', rec.name);
                                  onClose();
                                }}
                                disabled={isCurrent}
                                className={`text-xs px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg font-medium transition ${
                                  isCurrent
                                    ? 'bg-green-500/20 text-green-300 border border-green-500/30 cursor-default'
                                    : 'bg-white text-black hover:bg-gray-200'
                                }`}
                              >
                                {isCurrent ? 'Đang dùng' : 'Chọn dùng'}
                              </button>
                            </div>
                          ) : (
                            <button
                              onClick={() => handleStartPull(rec.name)}
                              disabled={isPullingThis || !ollamaAvailable}
                              className={`text-xs px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg font-medium flex items-center gap-1.5 transition ${
                                !ollamaAvailable 
                                  ? 'bg-white/5 text-gray-500 border border-white/5 cursor-not-allowed'
                                  : 'bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-500 text-white shadow-lg'
                              }`}
                              title={!ollamaAvailable ? 'Vui lòng khởi động Ollama trước' : 'Tải về máy qua Ollama'}
                            >
                              <Download size={13} />
                              {isPullingThis ? 'Đang tải...' : 'Tải về máy'}
                            </button>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 2: CÁC MODEL HIỆN CÓ TRÊN MÁY */}
          {activeTab === 'installed' && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">
                  Danh sách mô hình đã tải về lưu cục bộ trên máy tính của bạn:
                </p>
                <button
                  onClick={loadAllData}
                  className="text-xs text-blue-400 hover:underline flex items-center gap-1 font-medium"
                >
                  <RefreshCw size={11} className={loadingScan ? "animate-spin" : ""} /> Quét lại
                </button>
              </div>

              {localModels.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="p-8 text-center text-gray-500 text-xs border border-dashed border-white/10 rounded-xl space-y-3">
                  <Cpu size={28} className="mx-auto text-gray-600 opacity-70" />
                  <div>
                    Chưa phát hiện mô hình nào được cài đặt trong Ollama.<br />
                    Hãy chuyển sang tab <strong>"Gợi ý tải cho máy"</strong> để tải 1-click hoặc mở app Ollama.
                  </div>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setActiveTab('recommend')}
                    className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-green-500/20 text-green-300 border border-green-500/30 hover:bg-green-500/30 font-medium text-xs transition"
                  >
                    Xem gợi ý tải ngay
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  {localModels.map((m) => {
                    const isCurrent = currentProvider === 'ollama' && currentModel === m.name;
                    return (
                      <div
                        key={m.name}
                        onClick={() => {
                          onSelectModel('ollama', m.name);
                          onClose();
                        }}
                        className={`p-3.5 rounded-xl border transition-all cursor-pointer flex flex-col justify-between gap-2 ${
                          isCurrent
                            ? 'bg-green-500/15 border-green-500/40 text-green-300 font-medium shadow-md'
                            : 'bg-white/[0.03] border-white/10 hover:border-white/25 hover:bg-white/[0.06] text-gray-300'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                          <span className="font-semibold text-sm text-white truncate" title={m.name}>
                            {m.name}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono bg-white/10 text-gray-300 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">
                            {m.size_gb} GB
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-gray-500 pt-1 border-t border-white/5">
                          <span>{m.details?.family || 'gguf'}</span>
                          <span className="text-blue-400 font-medium flex items-center gap-1">
                            {isCurrent ? '✓ Đang sử dụng' : 'Bấm để dùng'}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 3: THÊM MODEL TÙY CHỈNH */}
          {activeTab === 'add_custom' && (
            <div className="space-y-5">
              <form onSubmit={handleAddCustomModel} className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10 space-y-3.5">
                <h3 className="text-xs font-semibold text-white uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Plus size={14} className="text-purple-400" /> Thêm mô hình mới
                </h3>
                
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] text-gray-400 mb-1">Nhà cung cấp (Provider):</label>
                    <select
                      value={customProvider}
                      onChange={(e) => setCustomProvider(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full bg-[#131314] border border-white/15 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-500"
                    >
                      <option value="ollama">Ollama (Local trên máy)</option>
                      <option value="9router">9Router AI Gateway</option>
                      <option value="openrouter">OpenRouter AI</option>
                      <option value="groq">Groq Cloud (Siêu tốc)</option>
                      <option value="openai">OpenAI (GPT-4o/o3...)</option>
                      <option value="gemini">Google Gemini</option>
                      <option value="custom">Tùy chỉnh khác (OpenAI-compatible)</option>
                    </select>
                  </div>

                  <div className="sm:col-span-2">
                    <label className="block text-[11px] text-gray-400 mb-1">
                      Tên Model ID / Model Name: <span className="text-red-400">*</span>
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      placeholder="Ví dụ: deepseek-r1:1.5b hoặc kr/claude-sonnet-4.5 hoặc gpt-4o"
                      value={customModelName}
                      onChange={(e) => setCustomModelName(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full bg-[#131314] border border-white/15 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-500"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 pt-1">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] text-gray-400 mb-1">Base URL (Tùy chọn, để trống dùng mặc định):</label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      placeholder={customProvider === 'ollama' ? 'http://127.0.0.1:11434' : 'Để trống nếu dùng mặc định'}
                      value={customBaseUrl}
                      onChange={(e) => setCustomBaseUrl(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full bg-[#131314] border border-white/15 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-500"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] text-gray-400 mb-1">API Key (Tùy chọn):</label>
                    <input
                      type="password"
                      placeholder="sk-..."
                      value={customApiKey}
                      onChange={(e) => setCustomApiKey(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full bg-[#131314] border border-white/15 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-500"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="pt-2 flex justify-end">
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-500 text-white font-medium text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5 transition shadow-lg shadow-purple-600/20"
                  >
                    <Plus size={13} /> Thêm & Dùng ngay cho Sổ tay
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>

              {/* Danh sách custom models đã lưu */}
              {savedCustomModels.length > 0 && (
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
                    Mô hình đã thêm gần đây ({savedCustomModels.length}):
                  </h4>
                  <div className="space-y-1.5">
                    {savedCustomModels.map((cm) => (
                      <div
                        key={cm.id}
                        onClick={() => {
                          onSelectModel(cm.provider, cm.model_name, cm.base_url, cm.api_key);
                          onClose();
                        }}
                        className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-white/[0.02] hover:bg-white/[0.06] border border-white/10 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 cursor-pointer transition"
                      >
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-300 border border-purple-500/30">
                            {cm.provider}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-xs text-white font-medium truncate">
                            {cm.model_name}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                          <span className="text-[11px] text-blue-400 hover:underline">Chọn</span>
                          <button
                            onClick={(e) => handleDeleteCustomModel(cm.id, e)}
                            className="p-1 text-gray-500 hover:text-red-400 rounded transition"
                            title="Xóa model này khỏi danh sách lưu"
                          >
                            <Trash2 size={13} />
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

        </div>

        {/* Modal Footer */}
        <div className="p-4 border-t border-white/10 flex items-center justify-between bg-white/[0.01]">
          <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span>Mô hình đang kích hoạt:</span>
            <strong className="text-green-400 font-mono">
              {currentProvider.toUpperCase()}: {currentModel || 'qwen2.5:3b'}
            </strong>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="px-4 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/15 text-white font-medium text-xs transition"
          >
            Đóng
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


# ==============================================================================
# FILE: frontend/src/components/QuizletModal.jsx
# ==============================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { 
  X, Layers, Play, Check, RotateCcw, Shuffle, Timer, Trophy, 
  ExternalLink, Copy, CheckCheck, Sparkles, ArrowRight, ArrowLeft,
  Flame, Award, AlertCircle, Clock, BrainCircuit
} from 'lucide-react';
import axios from 'axios';

export default function QuizletModal({ 
  isOpen, 
  onClose, 
  flashcards = [], 
  title = "Bộ thẻ học tập", 
  onOpenConfig,
  notebookId,
  isDark = true
}) {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState('flashcards'); // 'flashcards', 'srs', 'practice', 'match', 'export'

  // --- Flashcards State ---
  const [cards, setCards] = useState([]);
  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState(0);
  const [flipped, setFlipped] = useState(false);
  const [knownCards, setKnownCards] = useState(new Set());
  const [learningCards, setLearningCards] = useState(new Set());

  // --- SRS (Spaced Repetition) State ---
  const [srsData, setSrsData] = useState({});
  const [srsLoading, setSrsLoading] = useState(false);

  // --- Practice / Timer State ---
  const [practiceActive, setPracticeActive] = useState(false);
  const [practiceTimeLeft, setPracticeTimeLeft] = useState(60);
  const [practiceDuration, setPracticeDuration] = useState(60);
  const [practiceStreak, setPracticeStreak] = useState(0);
  const [practiceMaxStreak, setPracticeMaxStreak] = useState(0);
  const [practiceScore, setPracticeScore] = useState(0);
  const [practiceFinished, setPracticeFinished] = useState(false);
  const practiceTimerRef = useRef(null);

  // --- Match Game State ---
  const [matchTiles, setMatchTiles] = useState([]);
  const [selectedTile, setSelectedTile] = useState(null);
  const [matchedIds, setMatchedIds] = useState(new Set());
  const [wrongTileId, setWrongTileId] = useState(null);
  const [gameTimer, setGameTimer] = useState(0);
  const [gameActive, setGameActive] = useState(false);
  const [gameFinished, setGameFinished] = useState(false);
  const timerRef = useRef(null);

  // --- Export State ---
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (flashcards && flashcards.length > 0) {
      setCards([...flashcards]);
      setCurrentIndex(0);
      setFlipped(false);
      setKnownCards(new Set());
      setLearningCards(new Set());
    }
  }, [flashcards, isOpen]);

  // Fetch SRS progress from backend
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen && notebookId) {
      axios.get(`/api/notebooks/${notebookId}/srs`)
        .then(res => setSrsData(res.data || {}))
        .catch(err => console.error("Lỗi tải SRS:", err));
    }
  }, [isOpen, notebookId]);

  // Keyboard Navigation
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e) => {
      if (!isOpen || (mode !== 'flashcards' && mode !== 'srs' && mode !== 'practice')) return;
      if (e.code === 'Space') {
        e.preventDefault();
        setFlipped(prev => !prev);
      } else if (e.code === 'ArrowRight') {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleNext();
      } else if (e.code === 'ArrowLeft') {
        e.preventDefault();
        handlePrev();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [isOpen, mode, currentIndex, cards.length]);

  const handleNext = () => {
    if (currentIndex < cards.length - 1) {
      setCurrentIndex(prev => prev + 1);
      setFlipped(false);
    }
  };

  const handlePrev = () => {
    if (currentIndex > 0) {
      setCurrentIndex(prev => prev - 1);
      setFlipped(false);
    }
  };

  const handleShuffle = () => {
    const shuffled = [...cards].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
    setCards(shuffled);
    setCurrentIndex(0);
    setFlipped(false);
  };

  const markKnown = (idx) => {
    const nextKnown = new Set(knownCards);
    nextKnown.add(idx);
    setKnownCards(nextKnown);

    const nextLearning = new Set(learningCards);
    nextLearning.delete(idx);
    setLearningCards(nextLearning);

    handleNext();
  };

  const markLearning = (idx) => {
    const nextLearning = new Set(learningCards);
    nextLearning.add(idx);
    setLearningCards(nextLearning);

    const nextKnown = new Set(knownCards);
    nextKnown.delete(idx);
    setKnownCards(nextKnown);

    handleNext();
  };

  // --- SRS Review Handler ---
  const handleSrsReview = async (rating) => {
    if (!notebookId) return;
    setSrsLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await axios.post(`/api/notebooks/${notebookId}/srs/review`, {
        card_index: currentIndex,
        rating: rating
      });
      if (res.data && res.data.card) {
        setSrsData(prev => ({ ...prev, [currentIndex]: res.data.card }));
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
    } finally {
      setSrsLoading(false);
      handleNext();
    }
  };

  // --- Practice / Timer Game ---
  const startPractice = (durationSeconds = 60) => {
    setPracticeDuration(durationSeconds);
    setPracticeTimeLeft(durationSeconds);
    setPracticeScore(0);
    setPracticeStreak(0);
    setPracticeMaxStreak(0);
    setPracticeActive(true);
    setPracticeFinished(false);
    setCurrentIndex(0);
    setFlipped(false);

    if (practiceTimerRef.current) clearInterval(practiceTimerRef.current);
    practiceTimerRef.current = setInterval(() => {
      setPracticeTimeLeft(prev => {
        if (prev <= 1) {
          clearInterval(practiceTimerRef.current);
          setPracticeFinished(true);
          setPracticeActive(false);
          return 0;
        }
        return prev - 1;
      });
    }, 1000);
  };

  const handlePracticeAnswer = (correct) => {
    if (correct) {
      const newStreak = practiceStreak + 1;
      setPracticeStreak(newStreak);
      if (newStreak > practiceMaxStreak) setPracticeMaxStreak(newStreak);
      const points = 10 * (1 + Math.floor(newStreak / 3));
      setPracticeScore(prev => prev + points);
    } else {
      setPracticeStreak(0);
    }

    if (currentIndex < cards.length - 1) {
      setCurrentIndex(prev => prev + 1);
      setFlipped(false);
    } else {
      if (practiceTimerRef.current) clearInterval(practiceTimerRef.current);
      setPracticeFinished(true);
      setPracticeActive(false);
    }
  };

  // --- Match Game ---
  const startMatchGame = () => {
    const selected = cards.slice(0, 6);
    const tiles = [];
    selected.forEach((c, idx) => {
      tiles.push({ id: `front-${idx}`, pairId: idx, text: c.front, type: 'front' });
      tiles.push({ id: `back-${idx}`, pairId: idx, text: c.back, type: 'back' });
    });
    setMatchTiles(tiles.sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5));
    setSelectedTile(null);
    setMatchedIds(new Set());
    setWrongTileId(null);
    setGameTimer(0);
    setGameActive(true);
    setGameFinished(false);

    if (timerRef.current) clearInterval(timerRef.current);
    timerRef.current = setInterval(() => {
      setGameTimer(prev => prev + 1);
    }, 1000);
  };

  const handleTileClick = (tile) => {
    if (!gameActive || matchedIds.has(tile.pairId)) return;
    if (!selectedTile) {
      setSelectedTile(tile);
      return;
    }
    if (selectedTile.id === tile.id) {
      setSelectedTile(null);
      return;
    }
    if (selectedTile.pairId === tile.pairId && selectedTile.type !== tile.type) {
      const nextMatched = new Set(matchedIds);
      nextMatched.add(tile.pairId);
      setMatchedIds(nextMatched);
      setSelectedTile(null);
      if (nextMatched.size === Math.min(cards.length, 6)) {
        clearInterval(timerRef.current);
        setGameActive(false);
        setGameFinished(true);
      }
    } else {
      setWrongTileId(tile.id);
      setTimeout(() => {
        setWrongTileId(null);
        setSelectedTile(null);
      }, 600);
    }
  };

  const handleCopyExport = () => {
    const text = cards.map(c => `${c.front}\t${c.back}`).join('\n');
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
  };

  const currentCard = cards[currentIndex];
  const srsInfo = srsData[currentIndex];

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-md p-4 animate-fade-in font-sans">
      <div className={`w-full max-w-4xl h-[88vh] flex flex-col rounded-3xl shadow-2xl border overflow-hidden transition-colors ${
        isDark ? 'bg-[#121316] border-white/15 text-white' : 'bg-white border-gray-200 text-gray-900'
      }`}>
        
        {/* Header */}
        <div className={`p-4 border-b flex items-center justify-between ${
          isDark ? 'bg-white/[0.02] border-white/10' : 'bg-gray-50 border-gray-200'
        }`}>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="p-2 bg-gradient-to-tr from-purple-600 to-pink-600 rounded-xl text-white shadow-md">
              <Layers size={20} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-base font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
                Bộ thẻ & Luyện thi thông minh
                <span className="text-[11px] font-normal px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-400 border border-purple-500/30">
                  {cards.length} thẻ
                </span>
              </h2>
              <p className={`text-xs ${isDark ? 'text-gray-400' : 'text-gray-500'}`}>{title}</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {onOpenConfig && (
              <button
                onClick={onOpenConfig}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border border-white/15 text-purple-300 transition flex items-center gap-1.5"
                title="Tùy biến số lượng và độ khó của thẻ học"
              >
                <Sparkles size={13} />
                <span>Tùy chỉnh tạo thẻ</span>
              </button>
            )}
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className={`p-2 rounded-xl transition ${isDark ? 'hover:bg-white/10 text-gray-400 hover:text-white' : 'hover:bg-gray-100 text-gray-500 hover:text-black'}`}
            >
              <X size={20} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Tab Switcher */}
        <div className={`px-6 pt-3 border-b flex gap-4 overflow-x-auto scrollbar-hide text-xs font-semibold ${
          isDark ? 'border-white/10 bg-black/20' : 'border-gray-200 bg-gray-50'
        }`}>
          <button
            onClick={() => setMode('flashcards')}
            className={`pb-2.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 border-b-2 transition ${
              mode === 'flashcards' ? 'border-purple-500 text-purple-400' : 'border-transparent text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-200'
            }`}
          >
            <Layers size={14} /> Thẻ lật 3D
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setMode('srs')}
            className={`pb-2.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 border-b-2 transition ${
              mode === 'srs' ? 'border-indigo-500 text-indigo-400' : 'border-transparent text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-200'
            }`}
          >
            <BrainCircuit size={14} /> Ôn tập Anki (SRS)
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => { setMode('practice'); if (!practiceActive && !practiceFinished) startPractice(60); }}
            className={`pb-2.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 border-b-2 transition ${
              mode === 'practice' ? 'border-amber-500 text-amber-400' : 'border-transparent text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-200'
            }`}
          >
            <Timer size={14} /> Thi thử (Timer)
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => { setMode('match'); if (!gameActive && !gameFinished) startMatchGame(); }}
            className={`pb-2.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 border-b-2 transition ${
              mode === 'match' ? 'border-blue-500 text-blue-400' : 'border-transparent text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-200'
            }`}
          >
            <Play size={14} /> Trò chơi Ghép thẻ
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setMode('export')}
            className={`pb-2.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 border-b-2 transition ${
              mode === 'export' ? 'border-green-500 text-green-400' : 'border-transparent text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-200'
            }`}
          >
            <ExternalLink size={14} /> Xuất Quizlet.com
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Content Area */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">

          {/* MODE 1: STANDARD 3D FLASHCARDS */}
          {mode === 'flashcards' && (
            cards.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-gray-400 text-xs">Chưa có thẻ nào trong bộ học tập này.</div>
            ) : (
              <div className="w-full max-w-xl flex flex-col items-center gap-5">
                {/* Stats */}
                <div className="w-full flex justify-between text-xs text-gray-400 font-medium px-2">
                  <span>Thẻ {currentIndex + 1} / {cards.length}</span>
                  <div className="flex gap-4">
                    <span className="text-green-400">Đã nhớ: {knownCards.size}</span>
                    <span className="text-orange-400">Đang học: {learningCards.size}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* 3D Flip Card */}
                <div 
                  onClick={() => setFlipped(!flipped)}
                  className="w-full h-72 cursor-pointer perspective-1000 group select-none"
                >
                  <div className={`relative w-full h-full duration-500 transform-style-3d transition-transform ${flipped ? 'rotate-y-180' : ''}`}>
                    {/* FRONT */}
                    <div className="absolute inset-0 backface-hidden rounded-3xl border border-white/15 bg-gradient-to-br from-white/[0.08] to-white/[0.02] p-8 flex flex-col justify-between shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-sm">
                      <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs text-purple-400 font-bold tracking-wider uppercase">
                        <span>Mặt trước (Khái niệm)</span>
                        <span className="text-gray-500 font-normal">Nhấn để xem đáp án ↺</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold text-center text-white my-auto leading-relaxed">
                        {currentCard?.front}
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-center text-[11px] text-gray-500 font-mono">
                        (Phím cách để lật thẻ • Mũi tên trái/phải để chuyển)
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* BACK */}
                    <div className="absolute inset-0 backface-hidden rotate-y-180 rounded-3xl border border-purple-500/40 bg-gradient-to-br from-purple-900/30 to-black/60 p-8 flex flex-col justify-between shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-sm">
                      <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs text-purple-300 font-bold tracking-wider uppercase">
                        <span>Mặt sau (Giải nghĩa)</span>
                        <span className="text-gray-400 font-normal">Nhấn để quay lại ↺</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-sm md:text-base text-gray-200 text-center my-auto leading-relaxed overflow-y-auto max-h-44 scrollbar-hide">
                        {currentCard?.back}
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-center text-[11px] text-purple-400/80 font-mono">
                        ✓ Hãy tự đánh giá mức độ ghi nhớ bên dưới
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Navigation and Marking */}
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <button 
                    onClick={() => markLearning(currentIndex)}
                    className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold bg-orange-500/15 hover:bg-orange-500/25 text-orange-300 border border-orange-500/30 transition flex items-center gap-1.5"
                  >
                    <RotateCcw size={14} /> Chưa thuộc
                  </button>

                  <button onClick={handlePrev} disabled={currentIndex === 0} className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 disabled:opacity-30 transition">
                    <ArrowLeft size={16} />
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={handleShuffle} className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 transition" title="Xáo trộn thẻ">
                    <Shuffle size={16} />
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={handleNext} disabled={currentIndex === cards.length - 1} className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 disabled:opacity-30 transition">
                    <ArrowRight size={16} />
                  </button>

                  <button 
                    onClick={() => markKnown(currentIndex)}
                    className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold bg-green-500/15 hover:bg-green-500/25 text-green-300 border border-green-500/30 transition flex items-center gap-1.5"
                  >
                    <Check size={14} /> Đã nhớ
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )
          )}

          {/* MODE 2: ANKI SPACED REPETITION (SRS) */}
          {mode === 'srs' && (
            <div className="w-full max-w-xl flex flex-col items-center gap-5 animate-fade-in">
              <div className="w-full flex justify-between items-center text-xs text-gray-400 px-2 pb-1 border-b border-white/10">
                <span className="font-semibold text-indigo-400 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <BrainCircuit size={14} /> Ôn tập theo thuật toán SuperMemo-2 (SM-2)
                </span>
                <span className="font-mono">Thẻ {currentIndex + 1} / {cards.length}</span>
              </div>

              {/* Flashcard Card */}
              <div 
                onClick={() => setFlipped(!flipped)}
                className="w-full h-64 cursor-pointer rounded-3xl border border-indigo-500/30 bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-950/40 to-black/60 p-7 flex flex-col justify-between shadow-xl"
              >
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs">
                  <span className="text-indigo-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
                    {flipped ? "Định nghĩa / Giải thích" : "Khái niệm cần nhớ"}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-gray-500 text-[11px] font-mono">
                    {srsInfo ? `Đã ôn ${srsInfo.repetitions} lần • Khoảng cách: ${srsInfo.interval_days} ngày` : "Thẻ mới"}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-lg md:text-xl font-bold text-center text-white my-auto leading-relaxed">
                  {flipped ? currentCard?.back : currentCard?.front}
                </div>
                <div className="text-center text-[11px] text-gray-500">
                  {flipped ? "Bấm vào các nút bên dưới để ghi nhận tiến độ" : "Nhấn để lật xem đáp án"}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* 4 SRS Rating Buttons */}
              <div className="w-full grid grid-cols-4 gap-2 pt-2">
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleSrsReview(1)}
                  disabled={srsLoading}
                  className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-red-500/15 hover:bg-red-500/25 border border-red-500/30 text-red-300 text-xs font-bold transition flex flex-col items-center gap-1 shadow-sm"
                >
                  <span>Lại (Again)</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-normal text-red-400 font-mono">1 phút</span>
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleSrsReview(2)}
                  disabled={srsLoading}
                  className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-orange-500/15 hover:bg-orange-500/25 border border-orange-500/30 text-orange-300 text-xs font-bold transition flex flex-col items-center gap-1 shadow-sm"
                >
                  <span>Khó (Hard)</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-normal text-orange-400 font-mono">1 ngày</span>
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleSrsReview(3)}
                  disabled={srsLoading}
                  className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-blue-500/15 hover:bg-blue-500/25 border border-blue-500/30 text-blue-300 text-xs font-bold transition flex flex-col items-center gap-1 shadow-sm"
                >
                  <span>Tốt (Good)</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-normal text-blue-400 font-mono">3 ngày</span>
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleSrsReview(4)}
                  disabled={srsLoading}
                  className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-500/15 hover:bg-emerald-500/25 border border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-300 text-xs font-bold transition flex flex-col items-center gap-1 shadow-sm"
                >
                  <span>Dễ (Easy)</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-normal text-emerald-400 font-mono">7 ngày</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* MODE 3: PRACTICE MODE WITH TIMER & STREAKS */}
          {mode === 'practice' && (
            <div className="w-full max-w-xl animate-fade-in flex flex-col items-center">
              {practiceFinished ? (
                <div className="text-center py-8 space-y-4">
                  <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto shadow-xl">
                    <Trophy size={32} />
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white">Hoàn thành bài thi thử!</h3>
